Annexation

Cities taking land into their limits, usually at the owner's request.

59 governments we follow put 835 items about this on their agendas since January 2025; a vote passed on 138.

Found by fixed rules from each item's official wording (how). Newest first, by government. "On the agenda" means no vote is recorded online, which for many governments we follow is always the case. Follow this subject (RSS feed).

Village of Palm Springs (4)

  1. November 19, 2026

    New or changed city law (ordinance)

    (Second Reading/ Second Public Hearing) Ordinance No. 2026-07 - Voluntary Annexation — 1580 Prairie Road:

    What does “Ordinance” mean?

    A local law. Once adopted it applies to everyone in the city it covers. More

    Official wording

    (Second Reading/ Second Public Hearing) Ordinance No. 2026-07 - Voluntary Annexation — 1580 Prairie Road: Motion to approve the application submitted by Urban Design Studio, acting as agent for the applicants and contract purchaser, FL Residential Solution, LLC. The applicants are requesting the voluntary annexation of the property at 1580 Prairie Road into the Village of Palm Springs, pursuant to Section 171.044, Florida Statutes.

    On the agenda; no vote recorded.

    Official record: the meeting agenda.

  2. November 19, 2026

    Rezoning request at Small-Scale Land Use Amendment and Rezoning — 1580 Prairie Road:

    (Second Reading/ Second Public Hearing) Ordinance No. 2026-05 - Small-Scale Land Use Amendment and Rezoning — 1580 Prairie Road:

    What does “Rezoning” mean?

    A request to change the rules for what can be built on a piece of land. More

    Official wording

    (Second Reading/ Second Public Hearing) Ordinance No. 2026-05 - Small-Scale Land Use Amendment and Rezoning — 1580 Prairie Road: Motion to recommend approval of Ordinance No. 2026–05, a request for a Small-Scale Land-Use Amendment of the Future Land Use Map (FLUM) and Rezoning of 3.81 acres located at 1580 Prairie Road, which is currently developed with a 3,050 sf place of worship building and projected for redevelopment into a multifamily residential townhouse community. Voluntary annexation of the subject property is requested. ​

    On the agenda; no vote recorded.

    Official record: the meeting agenda.

  3. October 8, 2026

    (First Reading) Ordinance No. 2026-07 - Voluntary Annexation — 1580 Prairie Road:

    Official wording

    (First Reading) Ordinance No. 2026-07 - Voluntary Annexation — 1580 Prairie Road: Motion to approve the application submitted by Urban Design Studio, acting as agent for the applicants and contract purchaser, FL Residential Solution, LLC. The applicants are requesting the voluntary annexation of the property at 1580 Prairie Road into the Village of Palm Springs, pursuant to Section 171.044, Florida Statutes.

    On the agenda; no vote recorded.

    Official record: the meeting agenda.

Ordinance 43-26, First Reading, Voluntary Annexation B. Ordinance 44-26, First Reading, Small Scale Future Land Use (FLUM) amendment to assign…

    • Size: about 1.47 acres
    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The city's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. More

    Official wording

    Palmwood Residential - Applications for a 1.47+/- acre property located on the northwest corner of the intersection of Frederick Small Road and Palmwood Road, to request a: A. Ordinance 45-26, First Reading, Zoning Map amendment to assign TOJ Residential, Single-Family District (R-1). (Second Reading 12/15/26)

    On the agenda; no vote recorded.

City of Fernandina Beach (21)

  1. November 17, 2026

    VOLUNTARY ANNEXATION AGREEMENT - 1830 CLINCH DRIVE - RESOLUTION 2026-152 APPROVING THE EXTENSION OF WATER AND WASTEWATER SERVICES OUTSIDE THE CORPORATE LIMITS; ACCEPTING THE VOLUNTARY ANNEXATION PETITION FOR ONE PARCEL CONTAINING APPROXIMATELY 0.69 ACRES OF LAND LOCATED AT 1830 CLINCH DRIVE; AUTHORIZING ADMINISTRATIVE…

    Official wording

    Ordinance No. 2026-36; Ordinance No. 2026-37; Ordinance No. 2026-38; Ordinance No. 2026-39; Ordinance No. 2026-40; Ordinance No. 2026-41 The Pines PUD: (Second Reading/Public Hearing) Ordinances providing for Annexation, Comprehensive Plan Amendment *Major*, Comprehensive Plan Amendment *Minor*, and Zoning Map…

    • Size: about 424.82 acres
    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The city's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. More

    Official wording

    Ordinance No. 2026-36; Ordinance No. 2026-37; Ordinance No. 2026-38; Ordinance No. 2026-39; Ordinance No. 2026-40; Ordinance No. 2026-41 The Pines PUD: (Second Reading/Public Hearing) Ordinances providing for Annexation, Comprehensive Plan Amendment *Major*, Comprehensive Plan Amendment *Minor*, and Zoning Map Amendments, along with consideration of a Preliminary Development Plan, Development Agreement, and Tri-Party Sewer Agreement all on 424.82± acres undeveloped property located on the east side of the St. John's Heritage Parkway, south of the Melbourne Tillman C-63 canal, and north of the Melbourne Tillman C-54 canal, and west of the terminus of the Norfolk Parkway right-of-way. (Owners - D.R. Horton, Inc., F Carlyle Platt Family Limited Partnership and Charles Crawford, Errorhead, Inc.)     ORDINANCE NO. 2026-15 (INTERLOCAL SERVICE BOUNDARY AGREEMENT) AN ORDINANCE OF WELLINGTON, FLORIDA’S COUNCIL, ADOPTING AN INTERLOCAL SERVICE BOUNDARY AGREEMENT (ISBA) WITH PALM BEACH COUNTY TO COORDINATE FUTURE LAND USE, PUBLIC FACILITIES, AND SERVICES IN ADVANCE OF FUTURE ANNEXATIONS WITHIN THE AREA DEFINED BY THE…

    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The city's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. More

    Change to the future land-use map

    Request of Summer Time Management, LLC: To Annex the Property at 915 North Wymore Road and establish Commercial Future Land Use and Commercial (C-3) Zoning on the annexed property. (2nd reading)

    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The city's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. More

    Change to the future land-use map

    Ordinance 2024-02: Amends the Future Land Use Designation of 39.8 acres of Annexed Property at 155 State Road 207 from St. Johns County Residential-D to City Institutional. (A. Skinner, Director Planning and Building)

    • Size: about 39.8 acres
    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The city's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. More
